                        Fill: =RGBA(8, 144, 144, 1)
                        Font: =Font.'Segoe UI'
                        FontWeight: =FontWeight.Semibold
                        HoverBorderColor: =RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0)
                        HoverColor: =RGBA(255, 255, 255, 1)
                        HoverFill: =RGBA(0, 112, 112, 1)
                        PressedBorderColor: =RGBA(0, 0, 0, 0)
                        PressedColor: =RGBA(255, 255, 255, 1)
                        PressedFill: =RGBA(0, 112, 112, 1)
                        RadiusBottomLeft: =10
                        RadiusBottomRight: =10
                        RadiusTopLeft: =10
                        RadiusTopRight: =10
                        FillPortions: =2
                        Height: =56
                        OnSelect: |-
                          =If(IsBlank(DataCardValue1.Selected.Value) || IsBlank(DataCardValue2.Selected.Value),
                              Notify("Choose Approval For Delivery and Send For Repairs first.", NotificationType.Warning),
                              IsBlank(varWeekTilt) && !DataCardValue111.Value && !chkCabTiltNo.Value,
                              Notify("Answer " & DataCardKey111.Text & " first.", NotificationType.Warning),
                              IsBlank(varWeekTilt) && chkCabTiltNo.Value && IsBlank(txtCabTiltCmt.Text),
                              Notify("Add a comment for " & DataCardKey111.Text & ".", NotificationType.Warning),
                              IsBlank(varWeekGrease) && !DataCardValue49.Value && !chkGreasingNo.Value,
                              Notify("Answer " & DataCardKey49.Text & " first.", NotificationType.Warning),
                              IsBlank(varWeekGrease) && chkGreasingNo.Value && IsBlank(txtGreasingCmt.Text),
                              Notify("Add a comment for " & DataCardKey49.Text & ".", NotificationType.Warning),
                              IfError(
                                  Patch('Fleet Checklist', LookUp('Fleet Checklist', ID = varCheck.ID), {
                                      field_52: DataCardValue1.Selected,
                                      field_53: DataCardValue2.Selected,
                                      field_54: Coalesce(DataCardValue3.Text, User().FullName),
                                      field_55: Coalesce(DataCardValue4.SelectedDate, Today())
                                  });
                                  If(IsBlank(varWeekTilt), Patch('Fleet Checklist', LookUp('Fleet Checklist', ID = varCheck.ID), {CabTilt: {Value: If(DataCardValue111.Value, "Yes", "No")}}));
                                  If(IsBlank(varWeekTilt) && chkCabTiltNo.Value && IsBlank(LookUp(colCheckDefects, ItemField = "CabTilt")),
                                      Patch('GF Fleet Checklist Defects', Defaults('GF Fleet Checklist Defects'), {
                                          Title: DataCardKey111.Text, ChecklistID: varCheck.ID, Truck: varCheck.field_1,
                                          ItemField: "CabTilt", Comment: txtCabTiltCmt.Text}));
                                  If(IsBlank(varWeekGrease), Patch('Fleet Checklist', LookUp('Fleet Checklist', ID = varCheck.ID), {field_44: {Value: If(DataCardValue49.Value, "Yes", "No")}}));
                                  If(IsBlank(varWeekGrease) && chkGreasingNo.Value && IsBlank(LookUp(colCheckDefects, ItemField = "field_44")),
                                      Patch('GF Fleet Checklist Defects', Defaults('GF Fleet Checklist Defects'), {
                                          Title: DataCardKey49.Text, ChecklistID: varCheck.ID, Truck: varCheck.field_1,
                                          ItemField: "field_44", Comment: txtGreasingCmt.Text}));
                                  Notify("Sign-off saved", NotificationType.Success);
                                  Navigate(scrReview),
                                  Notify("Sign-off not saved: " & FirstError.Message, NotificationType.Error, 0)))
                        Size: =15
                        Text: ="Save sign-off"
                        Visible: =varReviewing && ctxPage >= CountRows(colPages)
